- **evtivity-conformance**: Test OCPP on EVtivity: send OCPP 1.6, 2.0.1 or 2.1 commands such as TriggerMessage, Reset or RequestStartTransaction and read the result, message and security logs, OCTT conformance tests and published results, 1.6 vs 2.1 differences. Not for simulated stations (evtivity-simulator) or a station that cannot connect (evtivity-troubleshoot). ([EVtivity/evtivity-skills](https://github.com/EVtivity/evtivity-skills) · 13 skills)
